!Sample_title = ROI 1, Patient 1, Primary tumor bed, MPR
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM9711715
!Sample_status = Public on Jun 29 2026
!Sample_submission_date = May 04 2026
!Sample_last_update_date = Jun 29 2026
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Non-small cell lung cancer, Primary tumor bed
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = tissue: Non-small cell lung cancer, Primary tumor bed
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = batch: 1
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = Interventions included pembrolizumab at a fixed dose of 200 mg combined with carboplatin (area under the curve 5) or cisplatin 75mg/m2, pemetrexed at 500 mg/m2 for nonsquamous cell carcinoma, and paclitaxel at 175 mg/m2 or nab-paclitaxel at 260 mg/m2 for squamous cell carcinoma. Surgery was performed after at least two cycles of neoadjuvant therapy. All patients received adjuvant pembrolizumab for up to one year after surgery. To ensure that all patients received four cycles of perioperative chemotherapy, adjuvant chemotherapy was given to patients who had not received adequate neoadjuvant therapy. The surgical approaches were chosen by the surgeon.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = Formalin-fixed and paraffin-embedded (FFPE) tissues were used to construct tissue microarrays (TMAs). Cores (2.0 mm in diameter) were punched from each FFPE block and arrayed across two recipient TMA blocks. Five-μm-thick TMA sections were deparaffinized and rehydrated, and regions of interest (ROIs) were selected based on immunofluorescence morphology markers.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA extraction was performed using the NanoString GeoMx Digital Spatial Profiler according to the manufacturer’s protocol. Briefly, TMA sections were processed and regions of interest (ROIs) were selected based on immunofluorescence morphology markers. Each ROI was sequentially decoupled from the oligonucleotides using ultraviolet (UV) light. UV-cleavable oligos coupled with individual genes were cleaved and collected from all ROIs using a digital mirror device-guided UV illumination. The resultant oligos were amplified by polymerase chain reaction (PCR) amplified (Bio-Rad Laboratories, Hercules, CA, USA) using GeoMx Seq Code primers for downstream library construction.
!Sample_description = Library name: ROI 1
!Sample_data_processing = The raw FASTQ files were run through GeoMx NGS pipelines to acquire digital count conversions and initial data files for further analysis.
!Sample_data_processing = The initial data were further processed using quality control (QC) and normalization. RNA data were normalized using Total Count normalization with a scaling factor set at 10,210.

The resultant oligos were amplified by polymerase chain reaction (PCR) amplified (Bio-Rad Laboratories, Hercules, CA, USA) using GeoMx Seq Code primers for downstream library construction.

!Sample_status = Public on Jun 29 2026
!Sample_submission_date = May 04 2026
!Sample_last_update_date = Jun 29 2026
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Non-small cell lung cancer, Primary tumor bed
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = tissue: Non-small cell lung cancer, Primary tumor bed
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = batch: 1
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = Interventions included pembrolizumab at a fixed dose of 200 mg combined with carboplatin (area under the curve 5) or cisplatin 75mg/m2, pemetrexed at 500 mg/m2 for nonsquamous cell carcinoma, and paclitaxel at 175 mg/m2 or nab-paclitaxel at 260 mg/m2 for squamous cell carcinoma. Surgery was performed after at least two cycles of neoadjuvant therapy. All patients received adjuvant pembrolizumab for up to one year after surgery. To ensure that all patients received four cycles of perioperative chemotherapy, adjuvant chemotherapy was given to patients who had not received adequate neoadjuvant therapy. The surgical approaches were chosen by the surgeon.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = Formalin-fixed and paraffin-embedded (FFPE) tissues were used to construct tissue microarrays (TMAs). Cores (2.0 mm in diameter) were punched from each FFPE block and arrayed across two recipient TMA blocks. Five-μm-thick TMA sections were deparaffinized and rehydrated, and regions of interest (ROIs) were selected based on immunofluorescence morphology markers.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA extraction was performed using the NanoString GeoMx Digital Spatial Profiler according to the manufacturer’s protocol. Briefly, TMA sections were processed and regions of interest (ROIs) were selected based on immunofluorescence morphology markers. Each ROI was sequentially decoupled from the oligonucleotides using ultraviolet (UV) light. UV-cleavable oligos coupled with individual genes were cleaved and collected from all ROIs using a digital mirror device-guided UV illumination. The resultant oligos were amplified by polymerase chain reaction (PCR) amplified (Bio-Rad Laboratories, Hercules, CA, USA) using GeoMx Seq Code primers for downstream library construction.
!Sample_description = Library name: ROI 16
!Sample_data_processing = The raw FASTQ files were run through GeoMx NGS pipelines to acquire digital count conversions and initial data files for further analysis.
!Sample_data_processing = The initial data were further processed using quality control (QC) and normalization. RNA data were normalized using Total Count normalization with a scaling factor set at 10,210.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: mm10
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: csv file include normalized count data

The resultant oligos were amplified by polymerase chain reaction (PCR) amplified (Bio-Rad Laboratories, Hercules, CA, USA) using GeoMx Seq Code primers for downstream library construction.

!Sample_status = Public on Jun 29 2026
!Sample_submission_date = May 04 2026
!Sample_last_update_date = Jun 29 2026
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Non-small cell lung cancer, Lymph node
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = tissue: Non-small cell lung cancer, Lymph node
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = batch: 1
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = Interventions included pembrolizumab at a fixed dose of 200 mg combined with carboplatin (area under the curve 5) or cisplatin 75mg/m2, pemetrexed at 500 mg/m2 for nonsquamous cell carcinoma, and paclitaxel at 175 mg/m2 or nab-paclitaxel at 260 mg/m2 for squamous cell carcinoma. Surgery was performed after at least two cycles of neoadjuvant therapy. All patients received adjuvant pembrolizumab for up to one year after surgery. To ensure that all patients received four cycles of perioperative chemotherapy, adjuvant chemotherapy was given to patients who had not received adequate neoadjuvant therapy. The surgical approaches were chosen by the surgeon.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = Formalin-fixed and paraffin-embedded (FFPE) tissues were used to construct tissue microarrays (TMAs). Cores (2.0 mm in diameter) were punched from each FFPE block and arrayed across two recipient TMA blocks. Five-μm-thick TMA sections were deparaffinized and rehydrated, and regions of interest (ROIs) were selected based on immunofluorescence morphology markers.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA extraction was performed using the NanoString GeoMx Digital Spatial Profiler according to the manufacturer’s protocol. Briefly, TMA sections were processed and regions of interest (ROIs) were selected based on immunofluorescence morphology markers. Each ROI was sequentially decoupled from the oligonucleotides using ultraviolet (UV) light. UV-cleavable oligos coupled with individual genes were cleaved and collected from all ROIs using a digital mirror device-guided UV illumination. The resultant oligos were amplified by polymerase chain reaction (PCR) amplified (Bio-Rad Laboratories, Hercules, CA, USA) using GeoMx Seq Code primers for downstream library construction.
!Sample_description = Library name: ROI 31
!Sample_data_processing = The raw FASTQ files were run through GeoMx NGS pipelines to acquire digital count conversions and initial data files for further analysis.
!Sample_data_processing = The initial data were further processed using quality control (QC) and normalization. RNA data were normalized using Total Count normalization with a scaling factor set at 10,210.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: mm10
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: csv file include normalized count data

The resultant oligos were amplified by polymerase chain reaction (PCR) amplified (Bio-Rad Laboratories, Hercules, CA, USA) using GeoMx Seq Code primers for downstream library construction.

!Sample_status = Public on Jun 29 2026
!Sample_submission_date = May 04 2026
!Sample_last_update_date = Jun 29 2026
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Non-small cell lung cancer, Primary tumor bed
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = tissue: Non-small cell lung cancer, Primary tumor bed
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = batch: 1
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = Interventions included pembrolizumab at a fixed dose of 200 mg combined with carboplatin (area under the curve 5) or cisplatin 75mg/m2, pemetrexed at 500 mg/m2 for nonsquamous cell carcinoma, and paclitaxel at 175 mg/m2 or nab-paclitaxel at 260 mg/m2 for squamous cell carcinoma. Surgery was performed after at least two cycles of neoadjuvant therapy. All patients received adjuvant pembrolizumab for up to one year after surgery. To ensure that all patients received four cycles of perioperative chemotherapy, adjuvant chemotherapy was given to patients who had not received adequate neoadjuvant therapy. The surgical approaches were chosen by the surgeon.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = Formalin-fixed and paraffin-embedded (FFPE) tissues were used to construct tissue microarrays (TMAs). Cores (2.0 mm in diameter) were punched from each FFPE block and arrayed across two recipient TMA blocks. Five-μm-thick TMA sections were deparaffinized and rehydrated, and regions of interest (ROIs) were selected based on immunofluorescence morphology markers.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA extraction was performed using the NanoString GeoMx Digital Spatial Profiler according to the manufacturer’s protocol. Briefly, TMA sections were processed and regions of interest (ROIs) were selected based on immunofluorescence morphology markers. Each ROI was sequentially decoupled from the oligonucleotides using ultraviolet (UV) light. UV-cleavable oligos coupled with individual genes were cleaved and collected from all ROIs using a digital mirror device-guided UV illumination. The resultant oligos were amplified by polymerase chain reaction (PCR) amplified (Bio-Rad Laboratories, Hercules, CA, USA) using GeoMx Seq Code primers for downstream library construction.
!Sample_description = Library name: ROI 46
!Sample_data_processing = The raw FASTQ files were run through GeoMx NGS pipelines to acquire digital count conversions and initial data files for further analysis.
!Sample_data_processing = The initial data were further processed using quality control (QC) and normalization. RNA data were normalized using Total Count normalization with a scaling factor set at 10,210.

The resultant oligos were amplified by polymerase chain reaction (PCR) amplified (Bio-Rad Laboratories, Hercules, CA, USA) using GeoMx Seq Code primers for downstream library construction.
!Sample_description = Library name: ROI 53

!Sample_status = Public on Jun 29 2026
!Sample_submission_date = May 04 2026
!Sample_last_update_date = Jun 29 2026
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Non-small cell lung cancer, Lymph node
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = tissue: Non-small cell lung cancer, Lymph node
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = batch: 1
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = Interventions included pembrolizumab at a fixed dose of 200 mg combined with carboplatin (area under the curve 5) or cisplatin 75mg/m2, pemetrexed at 500 mg/m2 for nonsquamous cell carcinoma, and paclitaxel at 175 mg/m2 or nab-paclitaxel at 260 mg/m2 for squamous cell carcinoma. Surgery was performed after at least two cycles of neoadjuvant therapy. All patients received adjuvant pembrolizumab for up to one year after surgery. To ensure that all patients received four cycles of perioperative chemotherapy, adjuvant chemotherapy was given to patients who had not received adequate neoadjuvant therapy. The surgical approaches were chosen by the surgeon.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = Formalin-fixed and paraffin-embedded (FFPE) tissues were used to construct tissue microarrays (TMAs). Cores (2.0 mm in diameter) were punched from each FFPE block and arrayed across two recipient TMA blocks. Five-μm-thick TMA sections were deparaffinized and rehydrated, and regions of interest (ROIs) were selected based on immunofluorescence morphology markers.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA extraction was performed using the NanoString GeoMx Digital Spatial Profiler according to the manufacturer’s protocol. Briefly, TMA sections were processed and regions of interest (ROIs) were selected based on immunofluorescence morphology markers. Each ROI was sequentially decoupled from the oligonucleotides using ultraviolet (UV) light. UV-cleavable oligos coupled with individual genes were cleaved and collected from all ROIs using a digital mirror device-guided UV illumination. The resultant oligos were amplified by polymerase chain reaction (PCR) amplified (Bio-Rad Laboratories, Hercules, CA, USA) using GeoMx Seq Code primers for downstream library construction.
!Sample_description = Library name: ROI 61
!Sample_data_processing = The raw FASTQ files were run through GeoMx NGS pipelines to acquire digital count conversions and initial data files for further analysis.
!Sample_data_processing = The initial data were further processed using quality control (QC) and normalization. RNA data were normalized using Total Count normalization with a scaling factor set at 10,210.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: mm10
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: csv file include normalized count data

The resultant oligos were amplified by polymerase chain reaction (PCR) amplified (Bio-Rad Laboratories, Hercules, CA, USA) using GeoMx Seq Code primers for downstream library construction.

!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Non-small cell lung cancer, Primary tumor bed
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = tissue: Non-small cell lung cancer, Primary tumor bed
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = batch: 2
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = Interventions included pembrolizumab at a fixed dose of 200 mg combined with carboplatin (area under the curve 5) or cisplatin 75mg/m2, pemetrexed at 500 mg/m2 for nonsquamous cell carcinoma, and paclitaxel at 175 mg/m2 or nab-paclitaxel at 260 mg/m2 for squamous cell carcinoma. Surgery was performed after at least two cycles of neoadjuvant therapy. All patients received adjuvant pembrolizumab for up to one year after surgery. To ensure that all patients received four cycles of perioperative chemotherapy, adjuvant chemotherapy was given to patients who had not received adequate neoadjuvant therapy. The surgical approaches were chosen by the surgeon.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = Formalin-fixed and paraffin-embedded (FFPE) tissues were used to construct tissue microarrays (TMAs). Cores (2.0 mm in diameter) were punched from each FFPE block and arrayed across two recipient TMA blocks. Five-μm-thick TMA sections were deparaffinized and rehydrated, and regions of interest (ROIs) were selected based on immunofluorescence morphology markers.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA extraction was performed using the NanoString GeoMx Digital Spatial Profiler according to the manufacturer’s protocol. Briefly, TMA sections were processed and regions of interest (ROIs) were selected based on immunofluorescence morphology markers. Each ROI was sequentially decoupled from the oligonucleotides using ultraviolet (UV) light. UV-cleavable oligos coupled with individual genes were cleaved and collected from all ROIs using a digital mirror device-guided UV illumination. The resultant oligos were amplified by polymerase chain reaction (PCR) amplified (Bio-Rad Laboratories, Hercules, CA, USA) using GeoMx Seq Code primers for downstream library construction.
!Sample_description = Library name: ROI 76
!Sample_data_processing = The raw FASTQ files were run through GeoMx NGS pipelines to acquire digital count conversions and initial data files for further analysis.

The resultant oligos were amplified by polymerase chain reaction (PCR) amplified (Bio-Rad Laboratories, Hercules, CA, USA) using GeoMx Seq Code primers for downstream library construction.
!Sample_description = Library name: ROI 83

!Sample_status = Public on Jun 29 2026
!Sample_submission_date = May 04 2026
!Sample_last_update_date = Jun 29 2026
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Non-small cell lung cancer, Lymph node
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = tissue: Non-small cell lung cancer, Lymph node
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = batch: 2
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = Interventions included pembrolizumab at a fixed dose of 200 mg combined with carboplatin (area under the curve 5) or cisplatin 75mg/m2, pemetrexed at 500 mg/m2 for nonsquamous cell carcinoma, and paclitaxel at 175 mg/m2 or nab-paclitaxel at 260 mg/m2 for squamous cell carcinoma. Surgery was performed after at least two cycles of neoadjuvant therapy. All patients received adjuvant pembrolizumab for up to one year after surgery. To ensure that all patients received four cycles of perioperative chemotherapy, adjuvant chemotherapy was given to patients who had not received adequate neoadjuvant therapy. The surgical approaches were chosen by the surgeon.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = Formalin-fixed and paraffin-embedded (FFPE) tissues were used to construct tissue microarrays (TMAs). Cores (2.0 mm in diameter) were punched from each FFPE block and arrayed across two recipient TMA blocks. Five-μm-thick TMA sections were deparaffinized and rehydrated, and regions of interest (ROIs) were selected based on immunofluorescence morphology markers.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA extraction was performed using the NanoString GeoMx Digital Spatial Profiler according to the manufacturer’s protocol. Briefly, TMA sections were processed and regions of interest (ROIs) were selected based on immunofluorescence morphology markers. Each ROI was sequentially decoupled from the oligonucleotides using ultraviolet (UV) light. UV-cleavable oligos coupled with individual genes were cleaved and collected from all ROIs using a digital mirror device-guided UV illumination. The resultant oligos were amplified by polymerase chain reaction (PCR) amplified (Bio-Rad Laboratories, Hercules, CA, USA) using GeoMx Seq Code primers for downstream library construction.
!Sample_description = Library name: ROI 91
!Sample_data_processing = The raw FASTQ files were run through GeoMx NGS pipelines to acquire digital count conversions and initial data files for further analysis.
!Sample_data_processing = The initial data were further processed using quality control (QC) and normalization. RNA data were normalized using Total Count normalization with a scaling factor set at 10,210.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: mm10
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: csv file include normalized count data

The resultant oligos were amplified by polymerase chain reaction (PCR) amplified (Bio-Rad Laboratories, Hercules, CA, USA) using GeoMx Seq Code primers for downstream library construction.

!Sample_status = Public on Jun 29 2026
!Sample_submission_date = May 04 2026
!Sample_last_update_date = Jun 29 2026
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Non-small cell lung cancer, Primary tumor bed
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = tissue: Non-small cell lung cancer, Primary tumor bed
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = batch: 2
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = Interventions included pembrolizumab at a fixed dose of 200 mg combined with carboplatin (area under the curve 5) or cisplatin 75mg/m2, pemetrexed at 500 mg/m2 for nonsquamous cell carcinoma, and paclitaxel at 175 mg/m2 or nab-paclitaxel at 260 mg/m2 for squamous cell carcinoma. Surgery was performed after at least two cycles of neoadjuvant therapy. All patients received adjuvant pembrolizumab for up to one year after surgery. To ensure that all patients received four cycles of perioperative chemotherapy, adjuvant chemotherapy was given to patients who had not received adequate neoadjuvant therapy. The surgical approaches were chosen by the surgeon.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = Formalin-fixed and paraffin-embedded (FFPE) tissues were used to construct tissue microarrays (TMAs). Cores (2.0 mm in diameter) were punched from each FFPE block and arrayed across two recipient TMA blocks. Five-μm-thick TMA sections were deparaffinized and rehydrated, and regions of interest (ROIs) were selected based on immunofluorescence morphology markers.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA extraction was performed using the NanoString GeoMx Digital Spatial Profiler according to the manufacturer’s protocol. Briefly, TMA sections were processed and regions of interest (ROIs) were selected based on immunofluorescence morphology markers. Each ROI was sequentially decoupled from the oligonucleotides using ultraviolet (UV) light. UV-cleavable oligos coupled with individual genes were cleaved and collected from all ROIs using a digital mirror device-guided UV illumination. The resultant oligos were amplified by polymerase chain reaction (PCR) amplified (Bio-Rad Laboratories, Hercules, CA, USA) using GeoMx Seq Code primers for downstream library construction.
!Sample_description = Library name: ROI 106
!Sample_data_processing = The raw FASTQ files were run through GeoMx NGS pipelines to acquire digital count conversions and initial data files for further analysis.
!Sample_data_processing = The initial data were further processed using quality control (QC) and normalization. RNA data were normalized using Total Count normalization with a scaling factor set at 10,210.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: mm10
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: csv file include normalized count data

The resultant oligos were amplified by polymerase chain reaction (PCR) amplified (Bio-Rad Laboratories, Hercules, CA, USA) using GeoMx Seq Code primers for downstream library construction.

!Sample_status = Public on Jun 29 2026
!Sample_submission_date = May 04 2026
!Sample_last_update_date = Jun 29 2026
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = Non-small cell lung cancer, Primary tumor bed
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = tissue: Non-small cell lung cancer, Primary tumor bed
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = batch: 2
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = Interventions included pembrolizumab at a fixed dose of 200 mg combined with carboplatin (area under the curve 5) or cisplatin 75mg/m2, pemetrexed at 500 mg/m2 for nonsquamous cell carcinoma, and paclitaxel at 175 mg/m2 or nab-paclitaxel at 260 mg/m2 for squamous cell carcinoma. Surgery was performed after at least two cycles of neoadjuvant therapy. All patients received adjuvant pembrolizumab for up to one year after surgery. To ensure that all patients received four cycles of perioperative chemotherapy, adjuvant chemotherapy was given to patients who had not received adequate neoadjuvant therapy. The surgical approaches were chosen by the surgeon.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = total RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = Formalin-fixed and paraffin-embedded (FFPE) tissues were used to construct tissue microarrays (TMAs). Cores (2.0 mm in diameter) were punched from each FFPE block and arrayed across two recipient TMA blocks. Five-μm-thick TMA sections were deparaffinized and rehydrated, and regions of interest (ROIs) were selected based on immunofluorescence morphology markers.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA extraction was performed using the NanoString GeoMx Digital Spatial Profiler according to the manufacturer’s protocol. Briefly, TMA sections were processed and regions of interest (ROIs) were selected based on immunofluorescence morphology markers. Each ROI was sequentially decoupled from the oligonucleotides using ultraviolet (UV) light. UV-cleavable oligos coupled with individual genes were cleaved and collected from all ROIs using a digital mirror device-guided UV illumination. The resultant oligos were amplified by polymerase chain reaction (PCR) amplified (Bio-Rad Laboratories, Hercules, CA, USA) using GeoMx Seq Code primers for downstream library construction.
!Sample_description = Library name: ROI 121
!Sample_data_processing = The raw FASTQ files were run through GeoMx NGS pipelines to acquire digital count conversions and initial data files for further analysis.
!Sample_data_processing = The initial data were further processed using quality control (QC) and normalization. RNA data were normalized using Total Count normalization with a scaling factor set at 10,210.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: mm10
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: csv file include normalized count data
